```python
import time
import math
import numpy as np

TARGET_UPPER = 1.5027
DEFAULT_N = 6121
MAX_DIRECT_N = 10000

_FFT_LEN_CACHE = {}


def _fft_len(length):
    length = int(length)
    if length in _FFT_LEN_CACHE:
        return _FFT_LEN_CACHE[length]
    val = int(np.fft.next_fast_len(length))
    _FFT_LEN_CACHE[length] = val
    return val


def clean_sequence(x):
    if x is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return arr


def convolution_normalized_arr(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a / s
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([float(a[0]) * float(a[0])], dtype=np.float64)
    out_len = 2 * n - 1
    nfft = _fft_len(out_len)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, nfft)
    b = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, nfft)[:out_len]
    np.maximum(b, 0.0, out=b)
    return b


def convolution_normalized(a):
    a = clean_sequence(a)
    if a is None:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    return convolution_normalized_arr(a)


def score_normalized_array(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    b = convolution_normalized_arr(a)
    if b.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * a.size * float(np.max(b)))


def score_array(a):
    a = clean_sequence(a)
    if a is None:
        return float("inf")
    return score_normalized_array(a)


def direct_score(a):
    a = clean_sequence(a)
    if a is None:
        return float("inf")
    n = a.size
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    if n <= MAX_DIRECT_N:
        b = np.convolve(a, a)
        mb = float(np.max(b))
        return float(2.0 * n * mb / (s * s))
    b = convolution_normalized(a)
    return float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(b)))


def normalize_with_mask(a, fixed_mask, floor):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        a = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    if fixed_mask is not None:
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        a[~fixed_mask] = 0.0
        if floor > 0.0:
            a[fixed_mask] = np.maximum(a[fixed_mask], float(floor))
    else:
        if floor > 0.0:
            a = np.maximum(a, float(floor))
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        if fixed_mask is not None and fixed_mask.any():
            a[fixed_mask] = 1.0
            a[~fixed_mask] = 0.0
            s = float(int(fixed_mask.sum()))
        else:
            a.fill(1.0)
            s = float(a.size)
        if s <= 0.0:
            a.fill(1.0)
            s = float(a.size)
    a = a / s
    if fixed_mask is not None:
        a[~fixed_mask] = 0.0
        s2 = float(a.sum())
        if s2 > 0.0:
            a = a / s2
    return a


def apply_floor_mask(a, fixed_mask, floor):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    if fixed_mask is not None:
        a[~fixed_mask] = 0.0
        if floor > 0.0:
            a[fixed_mask] = np.maximum(a[fixed_mask], float(floor))
    else:
        if floor > 0.0:
            a = np.maximum(a, float(floor))
    return a


def soft_weights(b, power):
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if b.size == 0:
        return b.copy()
    bb = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    if power <= 1.0:
        s = float(bb.sum())
        if s <= 0.0:
            return np.ones_like(bb, dtype=np.float64) / bb.size
        return bb / s
    eps = max(1e-300, float(bb.max()) * 1e-300)
    logw = power * np.log(bb + eps)
    logw = logw - float(logw.max())
    w = np.exp(logw)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones_like(bb, dtype=np.float64) / bb.size
    return w / s


def choose_active_indices(b, top_count, frac):
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if b.size == 0:
        return np.array([0], dtype=np.int64)
    mb = float(b.max())
    if mb <= 0.0:
        return np.array([0], dtype=np.int64)
    thr = max(1e-300, mb * float(frac))
    idx = np.where(b >= thr)[0]
    if idx.size == 0:
        return np.array([int(np.argmax(b))], dtype=np.int64)
    top_count = int(max(1, min(top_count, idx.size)))
    if idx.size > top_count:
        part = np.argpartition(b[idx], -top_count)[-top_count:]
        return idx[part]
    return idx


def active_gradient(a, indices):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k in indices:
        k = int(k)
        lo = k - (n - 1)
        if lo < 0:
            lo = 0
        hi = k if k < n else n - 1
        if lo > hi:
            continue
        h[k - hi:k - lo + 1] += a[lo:hi + 1][::-1]
    h = 2.0 * h
    h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
    return h


def dense_gradient(a, P):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    P = np.asarray(P, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a.copy()
    nfft = _fft_len(3 * n - 2)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(P, nfft)
    fr = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], nfft)
    hc = np.fft.irfft(fa * fr, nfft)
    h = 2.0 * hc[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
    return h


def make_random_sequence(n, rng):
    a = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.5, int(n)))
    trend = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n))
    a = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.5) * trend)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        a = np.ones(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(a.size)
    return a / s


def make_harmonic_sequence(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    m = int(rng.integers(2, 6))
    x = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for _ in range(m):
        freq = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 2.0))
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        amp = float(rng.uniform(-0.8, 0.8))
        x += amp * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * freq * idx / float(max(1, n)) + phase)
    std = float(np.std(x))
    if std <= 1e-12:
        x = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
    else:
        x = (x - float(x.mean())) / std
    a = 1.0 + 0.7 * np.tanh(x)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-10)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(a.size)
    return a / s


def active_optimize(a, time_budget, fixed_mask=None, seed=0, verbose=False):
    a = normalize_with_mask(a, fixed_mask, 0.0)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = score_normalized_array(a)
    time_budget = float(time_budget)
    if time_budget < 3.0:
        return best, best_score

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) if seed is not None else 0)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    tlimit = t0 + max(0.0, time_budget) - 1.0
    cur = a.copy()
    cur_score = best_score
    it = 0
    last_best = 0

    top_cycle = [120, 100, 80, 60, 50, 40, 30, 20, 15, 10, 8, 6, 5, 4, 3, 2, 2, 3, 5, 8]
    p_cycle = [2, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64, 128, 256, 512]
    total_bud = max(1.0, time_budget)

    while time.monotonic() < tlimit:
        if it > 0 and it - last_best > 400:
            cur = best.copy()
            sigma = float(rng.uniform(0.1, 0.4))
            cur = cur * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=cur.size))
            cur = apply_floor_mask(cur, fixed_mask, 1e-12)
            s = float(cur.sum())
            if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
                cur = cur / s
                cur_score = score_normalized_array(cur)
            last_best = it

        b = convolution_normalized_arr(cur)
        mb = float(b.max())
        cur_score = float(2.0 * cur.size * mb)
        if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best = cur.copy()
            best_score = cur_score
            last_best = it
            if verbose:
                print("  active improve it=%d score=%.9f" % (it, best_score))

        elapsed = (time.monotonic() - t0) / total_bud
        elapsed = min(1.0, max(0.0, elapsed))

        h = None
        hm = 0.0
        use_smooth = (it < 400) and (fixed_mask is None or it < 200)
        if use_smooth:
            power = p_cycle[min(len(p_cycle) - 1, it // 50)]
            P = soft_weights(b, power)
            h = dense_gradient(cur, P)
            hm = float(h.max())
            if hm <= 1e-300:
                h = None

        if h is None or hm <= 1e-300:
            top = int(top_cycle[min(len(top_cycle) - 1, it // 40)])
            frac = 0.98 if top <= 5 else 0.92
            idx = choose_active_indices(b, max(1, min(top, b.size)), frac)
            h = active_gradient(cur, idx)
            hm = float(h.max())

        accepted = False
        if h is not None and hm > 1e-300:
            if use_smooth:
                alphas = [0.2, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02]
            else:
                top = int(top_cycle[min(len(top_cycle) - 1, it // 40)])
                if top <= 3:
                    alphas = [1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 0.05]
                else:
                    alphas = [0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02]
            scale = hm
            for alpha in alphas:
                cand = cur * np.exp(-alpha * (h / scale))
                cand = apply_floor_mask(cand, fixed_mask, 1e-12)
                s = float(cand.sum())
                if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
                    continue
                cand = cand / s
                sc = score_normalized_array(cand)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur_score - 1e-11:
                    cur = cand
                    cur_score = sc
                    accepted = True
                    break

        if not accepted:
            sigma = float(rng.uniform(0.03, 0.35))
            cand = cur * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=cur.size))
            cand = apply_floor_mask(cand, fixed_mask, 1e-12)
            s = float(cand.sum())
            if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
                cand = cand / s
                sc = score_normalized_array(cand)
                if np.isfinite(sc):
                    therm = max(1e-5, 0.01 * (1.0 - elapsed)) * max(cur_score, 1e-6)
                    accept = False
                    if sc <= cur_score + 1e-12:
                        accept = True
                    elif sc <= cur_score + therm:
                        try:
                            if math.exp(-(sc - cur_score) / therm) > float(rng.random()):
                                accept = True
                        except Exception:
                            accept = False
                    if accept:
                        cur = cand
                        cur_score = sc
                    elif best_score < cur_score and rng.random() < 0.05:
                        cur = best.copy()
            else:
                if best_score < cur_score and rng.random() < 0.1:
                    cur = best.copy()

        it += 1
        if verbose and it % 250 == 0:
            print("    active iter=%d cur=%.9f best=%.9f elapsed=%.1f" %
                  (it, cur_score, best_score, time.monotonic() - t0))

    if cur_score < best_score:
        best = cur.copy()
        best_score = cur_score
    return best, best_score


def b2_state(n, limit):
    n = int(n)
    return {
        "n": n,
        "limit": int(limit),
        "sup": np.empty(n, dtype=np.int32),
        "pos": np.full(n, -1, dtype=np.int32),
        "in": np.zeros(n, dtype=bool),
        "c": np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.int32),
        "k": 0,
    }


def b2_add(st, x):
    x = int(x)
    n = st["n"]
    if x < 0 or x >= n or st["in"][x]:
        return
    k = st["k"]
    st["sup"][k] = x
    st["in"][x] = True
    st["pos"][x] = k
    if k > 0:
        idx = st["sup"][:k] + np.int32(x)
        st["c"][idx] += 2
    st["c"][2 * x] += 1
    st["k"] = k + 1


def b2_remove(st, x):
    x = int(x)
    k = st["k"]
    p = int(st["pos"][x])
    if x < 0 or x >= st["n"] or not st["in"][x] or p < 0 or p >= k:
        return
    idx = st["sup"][:k] + np.int32(x)
    st["c"][idx] -= 2
    st["c"][2 * x] += 1
    last = int(st["sup"][k - 1])
    st["in"][x] = False
    st["pos"][x] = -1
    if p != k - 1:
        st["sup"][p] = last
        st["pos"][last] = p
    st["k"] = k - 1


def b2_feasible_add(st, x, limit=None):
    n = st["n"]
    x = int(x)
    if x < 0 or x >= n or st["in"][x]:
        return False
    if limit is None:
        limit = st["limit"]
    limit = int(limit)
    if limit <= 0:
        return False
    c = st["c"]
    k = st["k"]
    if c[2 * x] > limit - 1:
        return False
    if k > 0:
        if limit <= 1:
            return False
        idx = st["sup"][:k] + np.int32(x)
        if int(np.max(c[idx])) > limit - 2:
            return False
    return True


def b2_conflict_element(st, s, rng):
    s = int(s)
    k = st["k"]
    n = st["n"]
    if k <= 0:
        return None
    sup = st["sup"]
    inb = st["in"]
    cands = []
    for i in range(k):
        y_prev = int(sup[i])
        z = s - y_prev
        if 0 <= z < n and inb[z]:
            cands.append(y_prev if y_prev <= z else z)
    if not cands:
        return None
    return int(cands[int(rng.integers(len(cands)))])


def b2_find_any_add(st, limit, rng, max_trials=1, full_scan=False):
    n = st["n"]
    k = st["k"]
    if k >= n:
        return False
    if k == 0:
        if full_scan:
            b2_add(st, 0)
        else:
            b2_add(st, int(rng.integers(n)))
        return True
    if not full_scan:
        for _ in range(int(max_trials)):
            x = int(rng.integers(n))
            if not st["in"][x] and b2_feasible_add(st, x, limit):
                b2_add(st, x)
                return True
        return False
    for x in range(n):
        if not st["in"][x] and b2_feasible_add(st, x, limit):
            b2_add(st, x)
            return True
    return False


def b2_binary_score(st):
    k = st["k"]
    if k <= 0:
        return float("inf")
    maxc = int(np.max(st["c"]))
    return float(2.0 * st["n"] * maxc / float(k * k))


def support_binary_score(support, n):
    support = np.asarray(support, dtype=np.int64).ravel()
    support = support[(support >= 0) & (support < n)]
    support = np.unique(support)
    k = int(support.size)
    if k == 0:
        return float("inf")
    counts = np.zeros(2 * int(n) - 1, dtype=np.int32)
    for x in support:
        counts[support + int(x)] += 1
    maxc = int(np.max(counts))
    return float(2.0 * n * maxc / float(k * k))


def b2_random_repair(n, limit, start_k, time_budget, rng):
    n = int(n)
    time_budget = float(time_budget)
    if time_budget <= 0.0:
        return None, float("inf")
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    tlimit = t0 + time_budget - 0.5
    st = b2_state(n, int(limit))
    start_k = int(start_k)
    if start_k < 1:
        return None, float("inf")
    if start_k > n:
        start_k = n
    perm = rng.permutation(n)
    added = 0
    for val in perm:
        x = int(val)
        if not st["in"][x]:
            b2_add(st, x)
            added += 1
            if added >= start_k:
                break

    best_support = None
    best_score = b2_binary_score(st)
    if st["k"] > 0:
        best_support = st["sup"][:st["k"]].copy()

    no_add = 0
    it = 0
    while time.monotonic() < tlimit:
        k = st["k"]
        score = b2_binary_score(st)
        if k > 0 and score < best_score:
            best_score = score
            best_support = st["sup"][:k].copy()

        if k == 0:
            b2_add(st, int(rng.integers(n)))
            no_add = 0
            it += 1
            continue

        maxc = int(np.max(st["c"]))
        if maxc > st["limit"]:
            s = int(np.argmax(st["c"]))
            y = b2_conflict_element(st, s, rng)
            if y is None:
                y = int(st["sup"][int(rng.integers(k))])
            b2_remove(st, y)
            b2_find_any_add(st, st["limit"], rng, 3)
            no_add = 0
            it += 1
            continue

        added_any = b2_find_any_add(st, st["limit"], rng, 15)
        if added_any:
            no_add = 0
        else:
            no_add += 1
            if no_add >= 25:
                no_add = 0
                if not b2_find_any_add(st, st["limit"], rng, 0, True):
                    old_k = st["k"]
                    s = int(np.argmax(st["c"]))
                    y = b2_conflict_element(st, s, rng)
                    if y is None and st["k"] > 0:
                        y = int(st["sup"][int(rng.integers(st["k"]))])
                    if y is not None and st["in"][y]:
                        b2_remove(st, y)
                        restored = False
                        for _ in range(30):
                            if b2_find_any_add(st, st["limit"], rng, 5):
                                if st["k"] >= old_k:
                                    restored = True
                                    break
                        if not restored and not st["in"][y]:
                            b2_add(st, y)
            elif no_add >= 8 and st["k"] > 0:
                no_add = 0
                old_k = st["k"]
                s = int(np.argmax(st["c"]))
                y = b2_conflict_element(st, s, rng)
                if y is None:
                    y = int(st["sup"][int(rng.integers(old_k))])
                if y is not None and st["in"][y]:
                    b2_remove(st, y)
                    restored = False
                    for _ in range(30):
                        if b2_find_any_add(st, st["limit"], rng, 5):
                            if st["k"] >= old_k:
                                restored = True
                                break
                    if not restored and not st["in"][y]:
                        b2_add(st, y)
        it += 1

    k = st["k"]
    score = b2_binary_score(st)
    if k > 0 and score < best_score:
        best_score = score
        best_support = st["sup"][:k].copy()
    return best_support, best_score


def b2_search(n, limit, time_budget, rng):
    n = int(n)
    time_budget = float(time_budget)
    if time_budget < 15.0:
        return None, float("inf")
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    tlimit = t0 + time_budget - 2.0
    best_support = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    base_start = int(math.sqrt(n) * (1.5 + 0.35 * float(limit)))
    base_start = max(30, min(n, base_start))
    sizes = []
    for factor in (0.8, 1.0, 1.2, 1.5):
        s = int(base_start * factor)
        s = max(20, min(n, s))
        if s not in sizes:
            sizes.append(s)

    r = 0
    while time.monotonic() + 10.0 < tlimit:
        ss = sizes[r % len(sizes)]
        ss = int(ss * (0.9 + 0.25 * float(rng.random())))
        ss = max(20, min(n, ss))
        chunk = min(60.0, tlimit - time.monotonic())
        sup, score = b2_random_repair(n, limit, ss, chunk, rng)
        if sup is not None and np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
            best_score = score
            best_support = sup.copy()
        r += 1
    return best_support, best_score


def balance_weights(support, n, time_budget, rng=None):
    n = int(n)
    support = np.asarray(support, dtype=np.int64).ravel()
    support = support[(support >= 0) & (support < n)]
    support = np.unique(support)
    k = int(support.size)
    if k < 3 or k > 1500:
        return None, float("inf")
    time_budget = float(time_budget)
    if time_budget < 5.0:
        return None, float("inf")
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(1)

    a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[support] = 1.0 / float(k)
    a = a / float(a.sum())
    cur = a.copy()
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = score_normalized_array(a)
    cur_score = best_score

    counts = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.int32)
    for x in support:
        counts[support + int(x)] += 1
    active_counts = counts > 0
    mach = int(np.count_nonzero(active_counts))
    if mach <= 0:
        return None, float("inf")
    flat = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
    flat[active_counts] = 1.0 / float(mach)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    tlimit = t0 + time_budget - 1.0
    it = 0
    while time.monotonic() < tlimit:
        b = convolution_normalized_arr(cur)
        cur_score = float(2.0 * n * float(b.max()))
        if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best = cur.copy()
            best_score = cur_score

        frac = min(1.0, 0.3 + 0.7 * float(it) / 500.0)
        target = (1.0 - frac) * b + frac * flat
        ts = float(target.sum())
        if ts > 0.0:
            target = target / ts

        logu = np.zeros(k, dtype=np.float64)
        for i, si in enumerate(support):
            sidx = support + int(si)
            bval = b[sidx]
            ratio = target[sidx] / np.maximum(bval, 1e-30)
            lr = np.log(np.clip(ratio, 1e-8, 1e8))
            logu[i] = 0.5 * float(np.sum(lr))
        logu -= float(np.mean(logu))
        logu = np.clip(logu, -2.0, 2.0)

        accepted = False
        for tau in (0.75, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1):
            w = cur[support] * np.exp(tau * logu)
            w = np.maximum(w, 1e-12)
            ws = float(w.sum())
            if ws <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ws):
                continue
            w = w / ws
            cand = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            cand[support] = w
            sc = score_normalized_array(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur_score - 1e-11:
                cur = cand
                cur_score = sc
                accepted = True
                break

        if not accepted:
            w = cur[support]
            w = w * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.1, size=w.size))
            w = np.maximum(w, 1e-12)
            ws = float(w.sum())
            if ws > 0.0 and np.isfinite(ws):
                cur[support] = w / ws
        it += 1

    if cur_score < best_score:
        best = cur.copy()
        best_score = cur_score
    return best, best_score


def _tuple_first(x):
    return x[0]


def record_best(store, arr, score):
    if arr is None or not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    arr = clean_sequence(arr)
    if arr is None:
        return
    score = float(score)
    if score < store.get("score", float("inf")):
        store["score"] = score
        store["seq"] = arr.copy()
    cands = store.get("cands")
    if cands is None:
        cands = []
        store["cands"] = cands
    if len(cands) < 12:
        cands.append((score, arr.copy()))
    else:
        max_idx = 0
        max_val = cands[0][0]
        for i in range(len(cands)):
            if cands[i][0] > max_val:
                max_val = cands[i][0]
                max_idx = i
        if score < max_val:
            cands[max_idx] = (score, arr.copy())


def final_select(store):
    candidates = []
    seq = store.get("seq")
    if seq is not None:
        candidates.append(seq)
    for pair in store.get("cands", []):
        candidates.append(pair[1])

    best = None
    best_score = float("inf")
    for s in candidates:
        if s is None:
            continue
        sc = direct_score(s)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best = s
            best_score = sc

    if best is None or not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best = np.ones(DEFAULT_N, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        best = np.asarray(best, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        best = np.clip(best, 0.0, 1000.0)

    s = float(best.sum())
    if best.size == 0 or s < 0.01:
        best = np.ones(DEFAULT_N, dtype=np.float64)
    return [float(x) for x in best]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    budget = float(budget_s)
    if budget <= 0.0:
        return [1.0]
    deadline = t_start + max(0.0, budget - 8.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) if seed is not None else 42)

    store = {"seq": None, "score": float("inf"), "cands": []}

    n = DEFAULT_N
    base_arr = None
    try:
        raw = height_sequence_1
    except Exception:
        raw = None

    if raw is not None:
        arr = clean_sequence(raw)
        if arr is not None and 20 <= arr.size <= 10000:
            base_arr = arr
            n = int(arr.size)

    initial_cands = []

    if base_arr is not None:
        sc = direct_score(base_arr)
        record_best(store, base_arr, sc)
        initial_cands.append({"a": base_arr.copy(), "mask": None, "score": sc})
        print("base score=%.9f n=%d" % (sc, n))

    for _ in range(2):
        arr = make_harmonic_sequence(n, rng)
        sc = direct_score(arr)
        record_best(store, arr, sc)
        initial_cands.append({"a": arr, "mask": None, "score": sc})

    arr = make_random_sequence(n, rng)
    sc = direct_score(arr)
    record_best(store, arr, sc)
    initial_cands.append({"a": arr, "mask": None, "score": sc})

    b2_top = []
    if budget > 150.0:
        b2_total = min(900.0, max(60.0, 0.32 * budget))
        if time.monotonic() + min(30.0, b2_total) < deadline:
            limits = [4, 3, 5]
            allocs = [0.40, 0.30, 0.30]
            for idx, lim in enumerate(limits):
                alloc = b2_total * allocs[idx]
                rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
                if rem < 25.0:
                    break
                rem_now = deadline - time.monotonic()
                alloc = min(alloc, max(0.0, b2_total * sum(allocs[idx:]) - (rem - rem_now)))
                if alloc < 10.0:
                    alloc = min(alloc, rem - 10.0)
                if alloc < 8.0:
                    continue
                sup, score = b2_search(n, lim, alloc, rng)
                if sup is not None and np.isfinite(score):
                    b2_top.append((score, sup))
                    seq = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                    seq[sup] = 1.0
                    record_best(store, seq, score)
                    initial_cands.append({"a": seq, "mask": None, "score": score})
                    mask = np.zeros(n, dtype=bool)
                    mask[sup] = True
                    initial_cands.append({"a": seq, "mask": mask, "score": score})
                    print("b2 limit=%d score=%.9f k=%d" % (lim, score, int(sup.size)))

    b2_top.sort(key=_tuple_first)
    balance_budget = min(300.0, 0.18 * budget)
    if balance_budget > 60.0 and b2_top:
        top2 = b2_top[:2]
        per = balance_budget / float(len(top2))
        for idx, pair in enumerate(top2):
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 40.0:
                break
            bscore, sup = pair
            t = min(per, rem - 30.0)
            if t < 20.0:
                continue
            bseq, bsc = balance_weights(sup, n, t, rng)
            if bseq is not None and np.isfinite(bsc):
                record_best(store, bseq, bsc)
                initial_cands.append({"a": bseq, "mask": None, "score": bsc})
                print("balanced top%d score=%.9f" % (idx, bsc))

    cand_sorted = []
    for idx, c in enumerate(initial_cands):
        cand_sorted.append((c["score"], idx, c))
    cand_sorted.sort(key=_tuple_first)

    chosen = []
    seen = []
    for sc, idx, c in cand_sorted:
        if len(chosen) >= 4:
            break
        key = (idx, c["mask"] is not None)
        if key in seen:
            continue
        seen.append(key)
        chosen.append(c)

    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    active_budget = max(0.0, remaining - 80.0)
    if active_budget > 50.0 and chosen:
        fractions = [0.35, 0.25, 0.20, 0.20]
        for idx, c in enumerate(chosen[:4]):
            t = active_budget * fractions[idx]
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 20.0:
                break
            t = min(t, rem - 30.0)
            if t < 8.0:
                continue
            sinit = int(rng.integers(0, 2**31 - 1))
            aseq, ascore = active_optimize(
                c["a"], t, fixed_mask=c.get("mask"), seed=sinit, verbose=False
            )
            if aseq is not None and np.isfinite(ascore):
                record_best(store, aseq, ascore)
                initial_cands.append({"a": aseq, "mask": None, "score": ascore})
                print("active candidate %d score=%.9f" % (idx, ascore))

        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 120.0 and store.get("seq") is not None:
            t = min(180.0, rem - 50.0)
            sinit = int(rng.integers(0, 2**31 - 1))
            aseq, ascore = active_optimize(
                store["seq"], t, fixed_mask=None, seed=sinit, verbose=False
            )
            if aseq is not None and np.isfinite(ascore):
                record_best(store, aseq, ascore)
                print("final polish score=%.9f" % ascore)

    if store.get("seq") is None:
        store["seq"] = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        store["score"] = 2.0

    return final_select(store)
```
